Copa Argentina 2016

According to the regulations, the following matches were also part of the Copa Argentina 2015/2016:
 462 championship matches of Campeonato Nacional B 2015.
 462 championship matches of Campeonato de Primera B 2015.
   4 matches of quarterfinals of Torneo Reducido of Campeonato de Primera B 2015.
 380 championship matches of Campeonato de Primera C 2015.
 240 championship matches of Campeonato de Primera D 2015.
 651 matches of First and Second Promotion of Torneo Federal A
1574 matches of Torneo Federal B, except Playoff Relegation.
All these 3773 matches shared with their respective championships.
 Anyway, to define the team to qualify for 2017 Copa Libertadores the following order was defined:
 1. Round reached.
 2. When teams reached the same round:
 2.1 If teams were from the same level: the best position in their 2016 championship.
 2.2 If teams were from different levels: playoff match in neutral field.
